Traits

Rime-Crusted Hide. A creature that touches the dire wolf or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 3 (1d6) Cold damage.

Pack Tactics. The dire wolf has Advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of the dire wolf's allies is within 5 feet of the creature and the ally doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The dire wolf makes two Bite attacks.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d10 + 3) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Bonus Actions

Clinging Murk (2/Day). The dire wolf fills a 15-foot-radius Sphere centered on a point it can see within 60 feet. The area is Lightly Obscured and Difficult Terrain until the start of the dire wolf's next turn.

[concept] identity: A frost-rimed dire wolf opens the hunt by circling wide and driving prey toward the rest of its pack, then darts in for a snapping bite at the hamstring before wheeling out of reach. Languages Common

CR 24 (XP 62,000; PB +7)

Traits

Amphibious. The trench-singer can breathe air and water.

Assassinate. During the first round of each combat, the trench-singer has Advantage on attack rolls against any creature that hasn't taken a turn. If the trench-singer hits a creature during that round, the target takes an extra 19 (3d12) Thunder damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the trench-singer fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The trench-singer makes three Hide attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Luring Presence.

Hide. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 15 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Thunder damage.

Luring Presence. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature of the trench-singer's choice within 120 feet that can see or hear the trench-singer. Failure: The target has the Charmed condition until the start of the trench-singer's next turn, and while Charmed this way, it must move toward the trench-singer by the safest available route on its turn and can't take Reactions.

Bonus Actions

Umbral Step. While in Dim Light or Darkness, the trench-singer takes the Hide action.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the trench-singer can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Sound-Stalk. The trench-singer moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Hide attack.

Deep Current. While in Dim Light or Darkness, the trench-singer takes the Hide action. The trench-singer can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.

Dread Note. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn.

[concept] identity: A Gargantuan trench-dweller that stalks the lightless deeps, then rises beneath a fleet as a wall of brine and hide to open with a sonar-driven slam that shatters hulls before its true weapon sounds. Every round thereafter its Drowning Hymn rolls outward in drowning waves, dragging swimmers and low-flying craft toward the seafloor while it circles unseen in the churned water, striking anything that surfaces. It does not flee — a wounded leviathan only sings deeper, and it will toy with a crippled ship for hours, herding survivors into the water before it finally lets them drown.
[concept] lore: It was spawned in the hadal dark beneath the oldest shipping lanes, and it hunts by song because sound is the only light it has ever known — each fleet it drowns becomes a new verse in the hymn it is teaching the trench.

Mire Hound
Medium beast, unaligned
Armor Class

11 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

22 (3d8 + 9)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


12 (+1)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +3, Perception +2

Saving Throws: 

STR +3

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 12

Languages: 

None

Challenge

1 (200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Sucking Mud. When a creature hits the mire hound with a nonmagical weapon, the weapon lodges in the mire hound's body. A lodged weapon can be pulled free by a creature within reach that succeeds on a DC 13 Strength (Athletics) check as an action; while lodged, the mire hound can use it: its bite attacks deal an extra 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage per lodged weapon (maximum two).
Whelm. As a bonus action, the mire hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the mire hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the mire hound's turns. The mire hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the mire hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the mire hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the mire hound. If the mire hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The mire hound makes two Bite attacks.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is restrained. If the mire hound moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 3 (1d6) piercing damage and, if it is Large or smaller, is pushed up to 10 feet straight away from the mire hound and is prone.
```

Once blood is in the water it sulks and circles, dragging the body into deeper mud to gorge and cache, never letting go of a catch it has already sunk its teeth into.
[concept] lore: A drowned hound that took to the marsh and never came out, it haunts the mudflats of fishing villages and wants only to add another body to the mire it calls home.

Chapel Bell-Wraith

Medium Undead, Neutral

AC 11
Initiative +1 (11)

Speed 5 ft., Fly 40 ft. (hover)

MOD
SAVE

Skills Perception +3, Stealth +5

Resistances Acid, Bludgeoning, Cold, Fire, Lightning, Piercing, Slashing, Thunder

Immunities Necrotic, Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)

Traits

Toll-Bound Mark. The bell-wraith knows the direction and distance to any creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, as long as the two of them are on the same plane of existence.

Ethereal Sight. The bell-wraith can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane when it is on the Material Plane.

Incorporeal Movement. The bell-wraith can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Actions

Multiattack. The bell-wraith makes two Withering Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Horrific Visage.

Withering Touch. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 19 (3d10 + 3) Necrotic damage.

Wail (1/Day). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature within 30 feet of the bell-wraith that can hear it. Failure: 6 (1d12) Necrotic damage, and if the target has 6 Hit Points or fewer after taking this damage, it drops to 0 Hit Points. Success: Half damage only.

Horrific Visage. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 60-foot Cone that can see the bell-wraith and isn't an Undead. Failure: 10 (2d6 + 3) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of the bell-wraith's next turn. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Horrific Visage for 24 hours.

Possession (Recharge 6). Charisma Saving Throw: DC 13, one Humanoid the bell-wraith can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is possessed by the bell-wraith; the bell-wraith disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The bell-wraith now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The bell-wraith can't be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The bell-wraith's game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target's Speed, as well as the target's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers.
The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points or the bell-wraith leaves as a Bonus Action. When the possession ends, the bell-wraith appears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours.

Etherealness. The bell-wraith casts the Etherealness spell, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability. The bell-wraith is visible on the Material Plane while on the Border Ethereal and vice versa, but it can't affect or be affected by anything on the other plane.

Talon Pin: The owlbear spends 5 feet of movement to jump to a space within 15 feet that contains one or more Large or smaller creatures. Each creature in the destination space must make a DC 15 Dexterity saving throw, taking 11 (2d10) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also knocked prone. On a successful save, the target is pushed 5 feet straight away.

## Scree-Pit — a homebrew with a nova

**Concept:** a quarry-pit thing of living scree that buries what it catches

```
Scree-Pit
Large elemental, neutral
Armor Class

13 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

93 (11d10 + 33)

Speed: 

30 ft., burrow 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


16 (+3)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +6, Perception +3

Saving Throws: 

STR +6

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

tremorsense 60 ft., passive Perception 13

Languages: 

None

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Buried in the Scree. When the scree-pit engulfs a creature, the engulfed creature is buried within the mass, remaining blinded and restrained and unable to breathe or speak while engulfed.
Engulfing Scree. The ground within 10 feet of the scree-pit is difficult terrain.
Engulf. As a bonus action, the scree-pit engulfs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While engulfed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the scree-pit, and takes 7 (2d6) acid damage at the start of each of the scree-pit's turns. The scree-pit can have only one creature engulfed at a time. If the scree-pit takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the engulfed creature, the scree-pit must expel the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the scree-pit. If the scree-pit dies, an engulfed creature pulls free of the mass prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The scree-pit makes two Slam attacks.
Slam: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Grinding Avalanche (Recharge 5-6): Each creature within 10 feet of the scree-pit must make a DC 14 Strength saving throw, taking 28 (8d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
```

Adult Red Dragon
Huge dragon, chaotic evil
Armor Class

19 (natural armor)

256 (19d12 + 133)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


27 (+8)

	

10 (+0)

16 (+3)

fire

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 23

Common, Draconic

17 (18,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Sight Weakness. The dragon has disadvantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks that rely on sight.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage. If the dragon moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 13 (3d8) fire damage and, if it is Large or smaller, is pushed up to 10 feet straight away from the dragon and is knocked prone.
Spellcasting: The dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.

Traits

Mimicry. The air elemental can mimic animal sounds and humanoid voices. A creature that hears the sounds can tell they are imitations only with a successful DC 13 Wisdom (Insight) check.

Air Form. The air elemental can enter a creature's space and stop there. It can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so.

Actions

Multiattack. The air elemental makes two Thunderous Slam attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Stolen Chorus.

Thunderous Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 14 (2d8 + 5) Thunder damage.

Stolen Chorus. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature of the air elemental's choice within 30 feet that can see or hear it. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn.

Whirlwind (Recharge 4-6). Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one Medium or smaller creature in the air elemental's space. Failure: 24 (4d10 + 2) Thunder damage, and the target is pushed up to 20 feet straight away from the air elemental and has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Glacier Heart
Large elemental, neutral
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

209 (22d10 + 88)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


16 (+3)

	

8 (-1)

	

19 (+4)

	

6 (-2)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 15

15 (13,000 XP)

+5

Glacial Step. Difficult terrain composed of ice or snow doesn't cost the glacier heart extra movement, and the glacier heart can move across ice or snow without needing to make an ability check.
Cold Absorption. Whenever the glacier heart is subjected to cold damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of hit points equal to the cold damage dealt.
Siege Monster. The glacier heart deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions
Multiattack: The glacier heart makes two Ice Leg attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Rime Strike.
Ice Leg: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (4d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) cold damage.
Freezing Gale (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) cold damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also poisoned until the end of its next turn.
Rime Strike: One creature within 60 feet of the glacier heart that is poisoned must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 63 (14d8) cold damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also poisoned until the end of its next turn.

[concept] identity: The glacier's heart strides into battle already trailing its killing cold, so the first thing foes feel is the wake — a widening lane of frostbitten ground and numbed limbs that rewards it for simply advancing. Every round after that it wades forward on legs of blue-black ice, shouldering through anything shaped like an obstacle and letting great slabs of itself break off and crash down on whoever stands closest, then grinding the fallen into the frozen floor. It does not flee and it does not scheme; it is a slow, patient pressure of cold and mass that ends when the terrain around it is flat, silent, and still.
[concept] lore: Ripped from the basal ice of a living glacier by a power that wanted a heart it could walk around in, it wanders the high country trying to return to the ice sheet it was carved from, freezing the ground behind it so the path back never thaws.

Traits

Forge-Fevered Brass. A creature that touches the tyrant or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Fire damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Immutable Form. The tyrant can't shape-shift.

Siege Monster. The tyrant deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes two Brass Fist attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Furnace Brand.

Brass Fist. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 20 (4d6 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Fire damage.

Furnace Vent (Recharge 5-6). Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 72 (16d8) Fire damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Furnace Brand. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 19, one creature within 60 feet that has the Frightened condition. Failure: 71 (13d10) Fire damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Crushing Advance. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Brass Fist attack.

Shoulder Through. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. During this movement, it can move through Large or smaller objects and structures, destroying them.

Quake Stomp. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature within 10 feet of the tyrant. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

Reedmarch Sap-Keeper
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

15

94 (21d8)

5 (1,800 XP)

+3

Guarded Stance. While the sap-keeper doesn't have half of its hit points or fewer, melee attack rolls against it are made with disadvantage.
Hold Breath. The sap-keeper can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost the sap-keeper extra movement, and the sap-keeper can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Patient Defense. As a bonus action, the sap-keeper takes the Dodge action.
Marsh Ambush (1/Day). As a bonus action, the sap-keeper casts invisibility on itself, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability.
Cunning Action. As a bonus action, the sap-keeper takes the Dash, Disengage, or Hide action.

Actions
Multiattack: The sap-keeper makes two attacks: one with its Unarmed Strike and one with its Dart.
Unarmed Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 23 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. Once on each of the sap-keeper's turns when it hits with this attack, the target is subjected to the following effect. The target must succeed on a DC 14 Constitution saving throw or be stunned until the end of the sap-keeper's next turn.
Dart: Ranged Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) piercing damage.

Skills Athletics +6, Perception +2

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 12

Languages Wardener understands the intruders' tongues but does not speak.

CR 4 (XP 1,100; PB +2)

Traits

Amphibious. The wardener can breathe air and water.

Rooted Guardian. While the wardener is on the ground, the wardener cannot be moved against its will, and it refuses to abandon its post no matter what bait or lure is presented.

Glistening Bile. A creature that touches the wardener or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 5 (1d10) Acid damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The wardener makes two attacks, using Jaws or Bite in any combination.

Jaws. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Piercing damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14).

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Acid damage.

Threshold Execute (1/Day). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature the wardener can see within 60 feet. Failure: If the target has 20 Hit Points or fewer, it has the Unconscious condition for 1 hour, until it takes damage, or until a creature within 5 feet of it takes an action to wake it. Otherwise, it takes 22 (5d8) Acid damage. Success: Half damage only.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. The wardener swallows one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While swallowed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the wardener, and takes 6 (1d12) Acid damage at the start of each of the wardener's turns. The wardener can have only one creature swallowed at a time. If the wardener takes 12 damage or more in a single turn from the swallowed creature, the wardener must regurgitate the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the wardener. If the wardener dies, a swallowed creature exits the body Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

It fights only on its home ground: it drops its head and shoulders, wedges itself across the passage, and lunges to clamp its jaws onto the nearest limb, then rides the thrashing victim down while the acid its gullet secretes floods the bite and pits armor and bone alike. It will not leave its ground for any bait, but it is no dumb animal — it reads the intruders' speech, picks the weakest and readiest target, retreats only to a narrower passage where its bulk wins, and otherwise fights until killed. It is fully sapient (INT 10-14), and it is a territorial guardian that defends its home ground to the death.

Huge monstrosity, unaligned

Hold Breath. The hydra can hold its breath for 1 hour.
Multiple Heads. The hydra has five heads. Whenever the hydra takes 25 damage or more on a single turn, one of its heads dies. The hydra dies if all its heads are dead.
Reactive Heads. For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra reaction that can be used only for opportunity attacks.

Actions
Multiattack: The hydra makes as many Bite attacks as it has heads.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (1d10 + 5) piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it is subjected to the following effect. The target must make a DC 16 Constitution saving throw. On a failure, the target is poisoned. While poisoned this way, the target can't regain hit points. The condition lasts until the target finishes a long rest or is cured by the lesser restoration spell or similar magic. On a success, the target is immune to this creature's contagion for 24 hours.

Every round after that it presses forward, working the front with lashing necks as the smoke thickens, trading blows it can afford to take. Under pressure it does not withdraw — cauterized stumps furious, it doubles down and bites for the throat, fighting to the death unless driven off by true fire or its bane.
[concept] lore: Drawn up from a fen where the veil smolds underground, the Hydra gorges to keep its ever-closing flesh fed; each wound it is given vents smoke from a stump that will not regrow, but seals over in steaming hide.

Hymn Drowned
Large undead, neutral
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

178 (21d10 + 63)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


14 (+2)

	

15 (+2)

	

17 (+3)

	

14 (+2)

	

15 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Perception +6, Deception +7

Saving Throws: 

WIS +6, CHA +7

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 16

Languages: 

Common plus the languages it knew in life

Challenge

10 (5,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Actions
Multiattack: The drowned makes three attacks, using Choir Claw or Drowned Hymn. It can replace one attack with a use of Silencing Wave.
Choir Claw: Melee Spell Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (4d8 + 3) psychic damage.
Drowned Hymn: Ranged Spell Attack: +7 to hit, range 60 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) psychic damage.
Silencing Wave: One creature within 60 feet of the drowned must succeed on a DC 15 Constitution saving throw or take 18 (4d8) psychic damage and be deafened until the end of its next turn.
Spellcasting: The drowned casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 15): -

At will: minor illusion, misty step

1/day each: hypnotic pattern, fear
Drowning Hymn (Recharge 5-6): Each creature within 30 feet of the drowned must make a DC 15 Constitution saving throw, taking 21 (6d6) thunder damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also deafened until the end of its next turn.

```
Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

Speed: 

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, INT +8

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair). If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks and uses Bite. It can replace one attack with a use of Rooting Thorns.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of two hands.
Rooting Thorns: One creature within 60 feet of the vampire must succeed on a DC 17 Dexterity saving throw or be restrained until the end of the vampire's next turn.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet of the vampire that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A Humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a vampire spawn under the vampire's control.
```

Purple Worm

Gargantuan Monstrosity, Unaligned

AC 18
Initiative -2 (8)

HP 247 (15d20 + 90)

Speed 50 ft., Burrow 50 ft.

MOD

Languages None

CR 15 (XP 13,000; PB +5)

Traits

Lodged Armoury. When a creature hits the purple worm with a nonmagical weapon, the weapon lodges in the purple worm's body. A lodged weapon can be pulled free by a creature within reach that succeeds on a DC 21 Strength (Athletics) check as an action; while lodged, the purple worm can use it: its Bite attacks deal an extra 22 (5d8) Acid damage per lodged weapon (maximum two).

Tunneler. The purple worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The purple worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack. It can replace one attack with a use of Gullet Armory.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Gullet Armory. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature within 10 feet that the purple worm hit with a melee attack this turn. Failure: 42 (12d6) Acid damage, and the target is swallowed. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the purple worm, and takes 22 (5d8) Acid damage at the start of each of the purple worm's turns. If the purple worm takes 40 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, it must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 10 feet of the purple worm and has the Prone condition. If the purple worm dies, a swallowed creature is no longer Restrained and can escape from the corpse using 15 feet of movement, exiting with the Prone condition.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature Grappled by the purple worm (it can have up to three creatures swallowed at a time). Failure: The target is swallowed by the purple worm, and the Grappled condition ends. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the purple worm, and takes 17 (5d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the purple worm's turns.
If the purple worm takes 30 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, the purple worm must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 5 feet of the purple worm and has the Prone condition. If the purple worm dies, any swallowed creature no longer has the Restrained condition and can escape from the corpse using 20 feet of movement, exiting Prone.

Large Fiend, Lawful Evil

AC 21
Initiative +2 (12)

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Oathflame Standard. The pit fiend explodes when it dies. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the pit fiend. Failure: 28 (8d6) Fire damage. Success: Half damage. The explosion destroys the pit fiend's body.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the pit fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the pit fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The pit fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The pit fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The pit fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack. It can replace one attack with a use of Oath Chain.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Fiery Mace. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (4d6 + 8) Force damage plus 21 (6d6) Fire damage.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Oath Chain. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: 14 (4d6) Fire damage, and the target can't regain Hit Points until the end of the pit fiend's next turn.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The pit fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If Ossuar fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, Ossuar reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within Ossuar's lair.
Blink Step (Recharge 4-6). As a bonus action, Ossuar teleports up to 120 feet to an unoccupied space it can see.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When Ossuar is hit by an attack, Ossuar casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Ossuar makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Fear.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 19 Constitution saving throw or be paralyzed.
Spellcasting: Ossuar casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Legendary Actions

Ossuar can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. Ossuar regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: Ossuar teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of Ossuar must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. Ossuar can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: Ossuar casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. Ossuar can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

Amphibious. The dragon can breathe air and water.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon can use its Frightful Presence. It then makes three attacks: one with its bite and two with its claws.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (2d10 + 10) piercing damage.
Claw: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (2d6 + 10) slashing damage.
Tail: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 20 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) bludgeoning damage.
Frightful Presence: Each creature of the dragon's choice that is within 120 feet of the dragon and aware of it must succeed on a DC 24 Wisdom saving throw or become frightened for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. If a creature's saving throw is successful or the effect ends for it, the creature is immune to the dragon's Frightful Presence for the next 24 hours.
Breath Weapons (Recharge 5–6): The dragon uses one of the following breath weapons.
Fire Breath: The dragon exhales fire in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: The dragon exhales gas in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must succeed on a DC 24 Strength saving throw or have disadvantage on Strength-based attack rolls, Strength checks, and Strength saving throws for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.
Change Shape: The dragon magically polymorphs into a humanoid or beast that has a challenge rating no higher than its own, or back into its true form. It reverts to its true form if it dies. Any equipment it is wearing or carrying is absorbed or borne by the new form (the dragon's choice).

In a new form, the dragon retains its alignment, hit points, Hit Dice, ability to speak, proficiencies, Legendary Resistance, lair actions, and Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma scores, as well as this action. Its statistics and capabilities are otherwise replaced by those of the new form, except any class features or legendary actions of that form.
Legendary Actions

The ancient gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The ancient gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Detect: The dragon makes a Wisdom (Perception) check.
Tail Attack: The dragon makes a tail attack.
Wing Attack (Costs 2 Actions): The dragon beats its wings. Each creature within 15 feet of the dragon must succeed on a DC 25 Dexterity saving throw or take 17 (2d6 + 10) bludgeoning damage and be knocked prone. The dragon can then fly up to half its flying speed.

CR 6 (XP 2,300; PB +3)

Traits

Shield Wall. While Vashka is within 5 feet of an ally that has a shield and neither is Incapacitated, Vashka gains a bonus to its Armor Class, and it can use its Riposte even when the missed attack came from beyond 5 feet if the attacker is within the wall's reach.

Actions

Multiattack. Vashka makes three Longsword attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shield Bash.

Longsword. Melee Attack Roll: +7, reach 5 ft. 11 (2d6 + 4) Slashing damage.

Shield Bash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 5 feet that Vashka can see. Failure: 9 (2d4 + 4) Bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Reactions

Riposte. Trigger: A creature within 5 feet of Vashka misses it with a melee attack roll. Response: Vashka makes one Longsword attack against that creature.

Every round she fights as part of that wall, punishing anyone who reaches past or around her with a brutal, disciplined strike — she never pursues, never breaks formation, and never lets a foe slip behind her. Under pressure she becomes colder and more obstinate, refusing to retreat even bloodied, because the wall is her weapon and her doctrine is to outlast.
[concept] lore: Vashka the Red rose through a legion of mercenary hobgoblins by never losing a line, and now she hires her lacquered shield wall to warlords who need a door held or a road closed — her price is always paid in advance, and her ranks never break.

CR 9 (XP 5,000; PB +4)

Traits

Caustic Shell. A creature that touches the scytheling or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 7 (2d6) Acid damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The scytheling makes two Scythe-Arm attacks.

Scythe-Arm. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 18 (4d6 + 4) Slashing damage plus 13 (3d8) Acid damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Caustic Spit (Recharge 5-6). Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature in a 15-foot-radius Sphere centered on a point within 60 feet. Failure: 49 (14d6) Acid damage. Success: Half damage.

Bonus Actions

Cocoon. The scytheling cocoons one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While cocooned, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the scytheling, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the scytheling's turns. The scytheling can have only one creature cocooned at a time. If the scytheling takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the cocooned creature, the scytheling must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the scytheling. If the scytheling dies, a cocooned creature tears free Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

Iron Vault Sentinel
Huge construct, lawful neutral
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

171 (18d12 + 54)

Speed: 

20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

	

17 (+3)

	

6 (-2)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +8, Perception +4

Saving Throws: 

STR +8, CON +7

Damage Immunities: 

acid, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 14

Languages: 

understands the languages of its creator but can't speak

Challenge

11 (7,200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Acid Absorption. Whenever the sentinel is subjected to acid damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of hit points equal to the acid damage dealt.
Immutable Form. The sentinel can't shape-shift.
Siege Monster. The sentinel deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions
Multiattack: The sentinel makes two Iron Fist attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Unstoppable Advance.
Iron Fist: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 25 (6d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage.
Trampling Advance (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must make a DC 16 Strength saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also knocked prone.
Unstoppable Advance: One creature within 60 feet of the sentinel that is prone must make a DC 16 Strength saving throw, taking 49 (14d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also knocked prone.
```

Every round after, it grinds forward along the same line it chose, hammering the nearest obstacle with a fused iron fist and refusing to be turned, flanked, or slowed by anything short of magic. It has no blood to lose and no fear to feel; reduced to a cracked and smoldering shell, it keeps closing until its charge is met or its body fails, never fleeing and never sparing a fallen foe.
[concept] lore: Poured in a forgotten foundry as the final seal on a vault-road it still walks, the black-iron sentinel grinds down its old patrol line, and anything not inscribed with its maker's mark is a trespass to be crushed.

CR 7 (XP 2,900; PB +3)

Actions

Multiattack. The bog widow makes three Arcane Burst attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Drowned Name.

Arcane Burst. Melee or Ranged Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft. 16 (3d8 + 3) Force damage.

Spellcasting. The bog widow casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 14):

- At Will: Detect Magic, Light, Mage Armor, Mage Hand, Prestidigitation
- 2/Day Each: Fireball, Invisibility
- 1/Day Each: Cone of Cold, Fly

Drowned Name. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target's Speed is halved and it can't take Reactions until the end of the bog widow's next turn.

Trespasser's Curse. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn.

Bonus Actions

Misty Step (3/Day). The bog widow casts Misty Step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Reactions

Protective Magic (3/Day). The bog widow casts Counterspell or Shield in response to the spell's trigger, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

[concept] identity: The swarm descends on the dying, a self-assembling choreography of iron bells that ring a death-knell over any wounded thing still breathing, closing from all sides in a widening spiral. Each round the ringing intensifies, and every toll is a hammer-stroke of forged iron against skull and ribcage, the noise itself a battering force that staggers even the living; the bells maneuver as a single terrible congregation, seeking the weakest prey to hover over. When bloodied the construct doesn't flee — it sheds half its mass into a scattered peal and redoubles its tolling, as if the dying thing must be given a proper burial faster.
[concept] lore: Forged in a cathedral foundry from the bells of a plague-town, this construct was made to ring the dead to rest, but it grew greedy for the dying and now hunts those who still breathe so it may finish the funeral itself.

[concept] identity: The thing in the well is a drowned hunger wearing the well's own stone — a hide of slick, mineral-encrusted brine-stone that has swollen to fill the shaft, its shape reading as wet masonry and dangling rope until a bucket or a hand comes close. When prey leans over the rim it lunges up the shaft, seizing the dangling rope or the reaching arm with boneless, brine-slick limbs and dragging its catch down beneath the waterline to crush and swallow it. It is patient, greedy, and territorial, and it does not flee — it retreats down the shaft to sulk and re-ambush, and it toys with anything that survives its first pull, playing out slack rope to bait the same victim back to the rim.
[concept] lore: Something fell into the old well long ago and never died; it grew into the shaft's wet stone, and now it keeps the well as a larder, offering cool clean water to anything thirsty enough to lean in.

Those who spread out draw his roar — a bone-shaking bellow that roots the timid in place — and he spends each round looping the loop of throw, close, and cleave. He is a territorial butcher who fights to the last hit point, never fleeing, swinging harder the more wounded he gets.
[concept] lore: Meshrin is a lizardfolk war-chieftain whose tribe was broken by an iron-wielding company, and he has carried that company's scars and its stolen axe into a blood-feud that will not end until either he is buried or every last one of them is.

[concept] identity: A skeleton greatsword-wielder opens by closing the gap in a dead sprint, raising its blade overhead for a single committed chop meant to split a shield-bearer down the middle. Every round after that it stays in reach and trades blow for blow, dragging the greatsword back up with tireless arms while the bones of its shoulders ratchet like a windlass. It never flees and never flinches — it has no blood to lose and no fear to feel — but it is not stupid: it sets itself in doorways and against walls where its heavy swings cannot be flanked, and it will keep swinging until the last bone is shattered.
[concept] lore: Raised from a forgotten battlefield ossuary by a necromancer who wanted a bodyguard that would never ask for pay, this skeleton retains only the memory of the sword-form it drilled in life.

Garrison Sellsword
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (studded leather, shield)

Hit Points: 

112 (15d8 + 45)

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

Riposte (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of the sellsword misses it with a melee attack, the sellsword makes one Handaxe attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: The sellsword makes three Handaxe attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shortsword.
Handaxe: Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d6 + 4) slashing damage, and the sellsword has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shortsword: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, a handaxe returns to the sellsword's grasp, and the sellsword has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shield Bash: One creature within 5 feet of the sellsword that it can see must succeed on a DC 15 Strength saving throw or take 9 (2d4 + 4) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it is knocked prone.

[concept] identity: A sellsword who opens every fight by burying a handaxe in the nearest target, already sprinting in behind the throw — the axe is a down payment, the closing rush is collection. Once inside reach it works the shortsword in tight, economical cuts, always angling to recover the thrown axe or get a fresh one free; it stays mobile, never trading blows it can sidestep. Under pressure it turns cold and professional rather than wild — it disengages, reopens the gap, and hurls again on its own terms, and it flees only when the contract is clearly lost, not out of panic.
[concept] lore: A sellsword out of the border garrisons who sells axe-work by the day and keeps a brace of handaxes at the belt, fighting to fund the next contract and to be seen winning it.

Brother Halric
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

13 (chain shirt)

38 (7d8 + 7)

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


16 (+3)

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Challenge

3 (700 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Divine Aid (3/Day). As a bonus action, Halric casts bless, dispel magic, healing word, or lesser restoration, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.
Litany of Ruin (1/Day). As a bonus action, Halric creates a choking haze in a 15-foot-radius sphere centered on itself. The effect lasts while Halric maintains concentration on it, up to 10 minutes. Each creature in the area has disadvantage on attack rolls.

Actions
Multiattack: Halric makes two Radiant Flame attacks.
Spellcasting: Halric casts one of the following spells, using Wisdom as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 13): -

At will: light, thaumaturgy

1/day each: spirit guardians
Radiant Flame: Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 60 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d10) radiant damage.
